## Construction and source mapping

All new declarations are under `YulangClosure.BrandedInventoryOwnerR64`.
`Carrier` is a **typed structural candidate**, not an authenticated interpretation
of Rust borrows: primitive tables for formals/recipes/local sources/constructions/
permissions/routes/plans/native rows and competitors are supplied as typed fields.
`owner` constructs exactly one R63 Owner from those fields; `call` selects the
unique owner-local source call and derives use/Catch/provider coordinates from
its record/schema. `complete_fields`, `call_coordinates` and `construct_exact`
state the exact equations for these same values. Source/native identity encodings
and record/table round trips are still missing, so these are not source closure.

`semanticFacts` stores endpoints separately from the full ordered provenance
edge list. `occurrences`, `admittedFacts` and `sourceFacts` preserve every per-fact
edge occurrence and its multiplicity; semantic IDs remain `.semantic store index`,
collected facts remain in the distinct collected namespace. No edge is deduplicated
or discarded to obtain success. `every_provenance_occurrence` and
`provenance_multiplicity` prove membership/length retention; collected facts,
permissions and every provider competitor also remain represented.

`RawView` represents all native TermView constructors: leaf, component, live
variable (kind/polarity/ordinal), positive bottom, negative top/bottom, both
functions with all four children, both variants with tag/payload lists, both
records with name/optional/value fields, and both tuples. `children` derives
children from these owning constructors. `termView` derives the R55 observation;
`raw_consumed_lookup_equations` proves both consumed readers
`positiveFunctionChild` and `negativeEntry` against unique reads of the same raw
owned terms, including missing and duplicate rows and every constructor case.
`seeds` is derived from components, formal native roots, every construction,
every plan target/use, and semantic-fact terms; no independent seed list is
assumed complete. `coverage_contains_seeds_and_children` is conditional on its
explicit finite `termCoverage` check. This check has not been proved true for an
actual Rust package; it is not a native receipt. `empty_derived_inventories`
retains legitimate empty tables without imposing a nonempty hypothesis.

Rust revision 2 (rehash matched) retains complete provenance, per-edge admitted
occurrences, owner-derived full TermView closure/child indices, and exact owner
call/schema fields. Its seed closure includes all collected endpoints and the
selected function/fact terms. The typed candidate has **not** established every
finite-table coordinate round trip, source-record projection or child-index
lookup for that actual structure. Carrier `Nat` identity fields and already typed
primitive source records leave those obligations visible. No full compiler,
allocator/failure, outer Run or whole-language instance is represented here.
